DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player guides an endlessly forward‑moving dot that can toggle between a minuscule point to slip through tight gaps and a massive sphere to smash obstacles, creating a rhythmic tension‑and‑release loop.

## Player
The avatar is a glowing dot that expands and contracts. Its only verb is **toggle size** (tap or press) which switches it between tiny and large forms; the dot always auto‑advances forward.

## Core loop
Every few seconds a new obstacle or passage appears. The player watches the upcoming geometry, decides whether to stay in the default size, shrink to thread a narrow gap, or enlarge to crush a barrier, then taps to toggle size. The cooldown after a large‑size smash forces a brief period of careful navigation before the next decision, keeping the forward momentum relentless.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Narrow gaps**: only traversable when the dot is in its tiny form.
- **Solid blocks**: block the path unless destroyed while the dot is large.
- **Moving sweepers**: horizontal or vertical walls that slide across the lane, requiring timing to avoid.
- **Spikes**: stationary lethal points that kill on contact with any size.
- **Overhead arches**: low ceilings that force a tiny form, otherwise cause a collision.

## Score
- **Survived obstacle**: each gap or barrier passed without collision.
- **Destroyed block**: each obstacle crushed while in large form.
- **Combo streak**: consecutive successful toggles without taking damage, rewarding skillful timing.

## Win and lose
- **Win**: Reach the end of the level or distance goal, triggering a celebratory burst of light and a final score tally.
- **Lose**: Collide with any obstacle while in the wrong size, causing the dot to explode and a game‑over screen displaying the final score.

## Feel
The game feels frantic yet elegant, with a steady forward thrust punctuated by sudden bursts of power. A neon‑glow aesthetic against a dark backdrop gives a retro‑futuristic vibe, while a pulsing synth‑chiptune soundtrack underscores each size change with satisfying beats and a crunchy smash sound when obstacles are destroyed.

## Why it's fun
The split‑second decision to shrink through a tiny opening or grow to obliterate a looming barrier delivers an instant, visceral “aha!” that makes players grin, curse, and keep trying for that perfect timing.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML scaffold & meta.json — create index.html with required structure and matching meta.json.
2. Core constants & state objects — define game constants and initialize player, hazards, score, and game state.
3. Animation loop skeleton — implement startLoop with requestAnimationFrame, cancel logic, and canvas clearing.
4. Input handling — set up keys Set, keydown/keyup listeners, and bind R key and restart button to reset.
5. Reset function — reinitialize all game variables, cancel prior loop, and start a fresh loop.
6. Player movement & auto‑advance — dt‑based forward motion, lateral movement, friction, and position updates.
7. Size toggle & cooldown — add tap/space handler to toggle size between tiny and large, enforce cooldown timer.
8. Hazard spawning system — create hazards with safe‑radius check, spawn timer, and random obstacle types.
9. Hazard movement & cleanup — update hazard positions each frame and remove off‑screen hazards.
10. Collision detection & game‑over transition — detect collisions based on current size, set state to "gameover" and stop gameplay.
11. Scoring mechanics — increase score on survived obstacles, destroyed blocks, and combos; update HUD.
12. Rendering — draw background, hazards, player (high contrast), and score HUD each frame.
13. Game‑over overlay — dim canvas, display "Game Over", final score, and restart instructions.
14. Observable contract — populate window.__game with state, score, player position/visibility, and objective each frame.
15. Audio feedback — implement lazy‑init beep() using AudioContext and play sounds for hits, scoring, and death.

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById('restart');

  let phase = 'playing'; // "playing" | "gameover"
  let score = 0;
  let lastTime = performance.now();
  let loopId = null;
  let keys = new Set();
  let audioCtx = null;

  // === RESET ==============================================================
  function reset() {
    phase = 'playing';
    score = 0;
    keys.clear();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  // === START LOOP =========================================================
  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('loop error', err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    // Placeholder: no game logic yet.
    // This function runs each frame.
    // dt is the elapsed time in seconds (capped at 0.1).
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    // Placeholder: draw a simple circle representing the dot.
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
  });
  document.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = 'sine';
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start();
      osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch (e) {
      // Audio may be unavailable in some environments.
    }
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase,
      score: score,
      player: {
        // No player entity in this placeholder; provide generic values.
        x: canvas.width / 2,
        y: canvas.height / 2,
        visible: true,
      },
      objective: 'Toggle size to navigate obstacles.',
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>

New version:

```
function reset() {
  // Cancel any existing loop
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  // Reset core state
  phase = 'playing';
  score = 0;
  keys.clear();
  lastTime = performance.now();
  // Reset player
  player.x = PLAYER_START_X;
  player.y = PLAYER_START_Y;
  player.radius = PLAYER_SMALL_R;
  player.size = 'small';
  player.sizeTimer = 0;
  player.cooldownTimer = 0;
  player.visible = true;
  // Reset hazards
  hazards = [];
  spawnTimer = 0;
  publishState();
  startLoop();
}
```

We also added cancelAnimationFrame at start to ensure loop cancelled (already done in startLoop but resetting before startLoop ensures no multiple loops).

Replace with new block.

Implement:

```
function update(dt) {
  if (phase !== 'playing') return;

  // Update player size timers
  if (player.sizeTimer > 0) {
    player.sizeTimer -= dt;
    if (player.sizeTimer <= 0) {
      // Revert to small size and start cooldown
      player.size = 'small';
      player.radius = PLAYER_SMALL_R;
      player.cooldownTimer = SIZE_COOLDOWN;
      beep(660, 0.1);
    }
  }
  if (player.cooldownTimer > 0) {
    player.cooldownTimer -= dt;
  }

  // Auto-advance forward (simulated by moving hazards leftward)
  // Spawn hazards
  spawnTimer -= dt;
  if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
    spawnHazard();
    spawnTimer = HAZARD_SPAWN_MIN + Math.random() * (HAZARD_SPAWN_MAX - HAZARD_SPAWN_MIN);
  }

  // Update hazards
  for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const h = hazards[i];
    h.x -= HAZARD_SPEED * dt;
    // Collision with player
    const closestX = Math.max(h.x, Math.min(player.x, h.x + h.w));
    const closestY = Math.max(h.y, Math.min(player.y, h.y + h.h));
    const dx = player.x - closestX;
    const dy = player.y - closestY;
    const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
    if (distSq < player.radius * player.radius) {
      if (player.size === 'large') {
        // Destroy hazard
        score += 10;
        beep(880, 0.05);
        hazards.splice(i, 1);
        continue;
      } else {
        // Player dies
        phase = 'gameover';
        beep(110, 0.5);
        // No need to process further hazards
        break;
      }
    }
    // Remove off-screen hazards
    if (h.x + h.w < 0) {
      hazards.splice(i, 1);
    }
  }

  // Increment score over time as a fallback
  score += Math.round(dt * 5);
}
```

Also need to define spawnHazard function somewhere, maybe in helpers section or at end of file.

function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }

// Spawn a new hazard at the right edge
function spawnHazard() {
  const w = 30 + Math.random() * 20;
  const h = 30 + Math.random() * 20;
  const y = Math.random() * (CANVAS_H - h);
  hazards.push({ x: CANVAS_W, y, w, h });
}

```
function render() {
  // Clear background
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // Draw hazards
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff52ae';
  for (const h of hazards) {
    ctx.fillRect(h.x, h.y, h.w, h.h);
  }

  // Draw player
  ctx.fillStyle = player.size === 'large' ? '#7df9ff' : '#ffffff';
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  // HUD
  ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
  ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);

  // Game over overlay
  if (phase === 'gameover') {
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,10,15,0.7)';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.font = '44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff52ae';
    ctx.fillText('Game Over', canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
    ctx.font = '20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
    ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
    ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#a5a5b8';
    ctx.fillText('Press R or click Restart', canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
  }
}

Common interpretations:
- "canvas appears blank" → the game is loading but `render()` isn't drawing anything. Check that `startLoop()` actually got called at boot, that `render()` runs every frame, and that you're issuing `ctx.fill*` / `ctx.stroke*` calls per entity each frame (not just clearing the canvas).
- "controls list mismatch" → the `<ul class="game-controls">` in `index.html` and the `controls` array in `meta.json` disagree. Pick one source of truth, edit the other to match exactly.
- "playtest: player.visible was false for X% of frames" → the camera/position math is letting the player drift outside `[0, canvas.width) × [0, canvas.height)`. Either clamp the player or update `player.visible` to reflect the truth (don't just hardcode `true`).
- "playtest: state took invalid value" → `window.__game.state` must be exactly `"menu"`, `"playing"`, or `"gameover"`. No other strings.
- "Required file missing: meta.json" → you didn't write the file. Use your write tool now.
- "pageerror" / "console.error" → an exception is being thrown at runtime. Read the message, locate the line, fix the bug.

New update function code (indentation 2 spaces before `function` line). Let's construct:

```
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== 'playing') return;

    // Update size toggle timers
    if (player.largeTimer > 0) {
      player.largeTimer -= dt;
      if (player.largeTimer <= 0) {
        // revert to small size and start cooldown
        player.isLarge = false;
        player.radius = PLAYER_SMALL_R;
        player.largeTimer = 0;
        player.cooldownTimer = SIZE_COOLDOWN;
      }
    }
    if (player.cooldownTimer > 0) {
      player.cooldownTimer -= dt;
      if (player.cooldownTimer < 0) player.cooldownTimer = 0;
    }

    // Player vertical movement (up/down)
    const dirY = (keys.has('ArrowDown') || keys.has('s') || keys.has('S') ? 1 : 0)
               - (keys.has('ArrowUp') || keys.has('w') || keys.has('W') ? 1 : 0);
    player.y += dirY * PLAYER_SPEED * dt;
    // Clamp within canvas
    player.y = clamp(player.y, player.radius, canvas.height - player.radius);

    // Spawn hazards
    spawnTimer -= dt;
    if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
      spawnHazard();
      spawnTimer = Math.random() * (HAZARD_SPAWN_MAX - HAZARD_SPAWN_MIN) + HAZARD_SPAWN_MIN;
    }

    // Update hazards positions and handle collisions
    for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const h = hazards[i];
      h.x -= HAZARD_SPEED * dt;
      // Collision detection
      const collides = !(player.x + player.radius < h.x ||
                         player.x - player.radius > h.x + HAZARD_W ||
                         player.y + player.radius < h.y ||
                         player.y - player.radius > h.y + HAZARD_H);
      if (collides) {
        if (player.isLarge) {
          // Destroy block, score
          score += 10;
          beep(880, 0.05);
          hazards.splice(i, 1);
          continue;
        } else {
          // Game over
          phase = 'gameover';
          beep(110, 0.25);
          break;
        }
      }
      // Remove off-screen hazards
      if (h.x + HAZARD_W < 0) {
        hazards.splice(i, 1);
        // Scoring for survived hazard
        if (phase === 'playing') {
          score += 5;
          beep(440, 0.04);
        }
      }
    }
  }
```

We need a function spawnHazard defined elsewhere, maybe after UPDATE or somewhere else, but we can define it in a new helper section or before update.

Simplify: define function spawnHazard.

Add after UPDATE (or before) a new function.

We'll locate where to insert: after UPDATE, before RENDER perhaps.

We'll add new function:

```
  function spawnHazard() {
    // Choose a random y position, ensure safe radius from player start
    let y;
    let attempts = 0;
    do {
      y = Math.random() * (canvas.height - HAZARD_H);
      attempts++;
      if (attempts > 10) break;
    } while (Math.hypot(PLAYER_START_X - canvas.width, y - PLAYER_START_Y) < SAFE_RADIUS);
    hazards.push({ x: canvas.width, y, w: HAZARD_W, h: HAZARD_H });
  }
```

But more appropriate safe check: The hazard spawns at right edge (canvas.width). Distance from player position to spawn point: we can approximate that at spawn it is far; safe radius check probably more for immediate spawn near player for early time. We'll just spawn at right edge; no need safe radius; but to meet spec we may include check.

Now update render function: replace placeholder with full drawing.

We'll replace entire section with new render code:

```
  function render() {
    // Clear background
    ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

    // Draw hazards
    for (const h of hazards) {
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff52ae';
      ctx.fillRect(h.x, h.y, h.w, h.h);
    }

    // Draw player
    ctx.fillStyle = player.isLarge ? '#ff52ae' : '#7df9ff';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    // HUD
    ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
    ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = 'right';
    ctx.fillText(`Size ${player.isLarge ? 'Large' : 'Small'}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);

    // Game over overlay
    if (phase === 'gameover') {
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,10,15,0.7)';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.font = '44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff52ae';
      ctx.fillText('Game Over', canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
      ctx.font = '20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
      ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
      ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
      ctx.fillStyle = '#a5a5b8';
      ctx.fillText('Press R or click Restart', canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
    }
  }
```

prompt (13,853 chars)
## Reference example: "breakout"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Breaker",
  "description": "Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.",
  "controls": [
    "Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle",
    "Hold Space to launch faster",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "breakout", "bouncing"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Breaker</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #0f1320; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Breaker</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle</li>
      <li>Hold Space to launch faster</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const PADDLE_W = 110;
  const PADDLE_H = 14;
  const PADDLE_Y = 560;
  const PADDLE_SPEED = 700;       // px/sec

  const BALL_R = 7;
  const BALL_BASE_SPEED = 360;    // px/sec at start
  const BALL_MAX_SPEED  = 720;
  const BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT = 6; // px/sec added per brick

  const BRICK_ROWS = 6;
  const BRICK_COLS = 12;
  const BRICK_W = 60;
  const BRICK_H = 20;
  const BRICK_GAP = 4;
  const BRICK_TOP = 60;
  const BRICK_LEFT = (800 - (BRICK_COLS * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP) - BRICK_GAP)) / 2;
  const ROW_COLORS = ["#ff52ae", "#ff8c52", "#ffd23f", "#a3ff52", "#52ffae", "#7df9ff"];

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let paddle;        // { x } center x
  let ball;          // { x, y, vx, vy, speed }
  let bricks;        // array of { x, y, w, h, color, alive }
  let score;
  let bricksLeft;
  let phase;         // "playing" | "won" | "lost"
  let keys;          // pressed key set
  let highScore = 0;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    paddle = { x: 400 };
    ball = { x: 400, y: 480, vx: 0, vy: 0, speed: BALL_BASE_SPEED };
    launchBall();
    bricks = [];
    for (let r = 0; r < BRICK_ROWS; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < BRICK_COLS; c++) {
        bricks.push({
          x: BRICK_LEFT + c * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP),
          y: BRICK_TOP + r * (BRICK_H + BRICK_GAP),
          w: BRICK_W, h: BRICK_H,
          color: ROW_COLORS[r % ROW_COLORS.length],
          alive: true,
        });
      }
    }
    bricksLeft = bricks.length;
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function launchBall() {
    // Always launch upward, mild horizontal lean either side.
    const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + (Math.random() * 0.6 - 0.3);
    ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
    ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Paddle.
    const dirX = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
               - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
    const speedMul = keys.has(" ") ? 1.6 : 1;
    paddle.x += dirX * PADDLE_SPEED * speedMul * dt;
    paddle.x = clamp(paddle.x, PADDLE_W / 2, canvas.width - PADDLE_W / 2);

    // Ball motion.
    ball.x += ball.vx * dt;
    ball.y += ball.vy * dt;

    // Walls.
    if (ball.x < BALL_R) { ball.x = BALL_R; ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.x > canvas.width - BALL_R) { ball.x = canvas.width - BALL_R; ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.y < BALL_R) { ball.y = BALL_R; ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy); beep(220, 0.04); }

    // Floor — lose.
    if (ball.y > canvas.height + BALL_R) {
      phase = "lost";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(110, 0.25);
      return;
    }

    // Paddle.
    const px = paddle.x, py = PADDLE_Y;
    if (ball.vy > 0
        && ball.y + BALL_R >= py
        && ball.y - BALL_R <= py + PADDLE_H
        && ball.x >= px - PADDLE_W / 2 - BALL_R
        && ball.x <= px + PADDLE_W / 2 + BALL_R) {
      // Bounce direction depends on where it hit on the paddle.
      const offset = (ball.x - px) / (PADDLE_W / 2); // -1..1
      const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + offset * 1.0;   // up-ish, leaning side
      ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.y = py - BALL_R - 0.1;
      beep(540, 0.05);
    }

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      if (ball.x + BALL_R < b.x || ball.x - BALL_R > b.x + b.w) continue;
      if (ball.y + BALL_R < b.y || ball.y - BALL_R > b.y + b.h) continue;

      b.alive = false;
      bricksLeft--;
      score += 10;
      ball.speed = Math.min(BALL_MAX_SPEED, ball.speed + BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT);

      // Decide which side was hit by comparing penetrations.
      const overlapX = Math.min(ball.x + BALL_R - b.x, b.x + b.w - (ball.x - BALL_R));
      const overlapY = Math.min(ball.y + BALL_R - b.y, b.y + b.h - (ball.y - BALL_R));
      if (overlapX < overlapY) ball.vx = -ball.vx; else ball.vy = -ball.vy;

      // Renormalize to current speed.
      const m = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
      ball.vx = (ball.vx / m) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = (ball.vy / m) * ball.speed;
      beep(880, 0.05);
      break;
    }

    if (bricksLeft === 0) {
      phase = "won";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(990, 0.2);
    }
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
      ctx.fillRect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
    }

    // Paddle.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.fillRect(paddle.x - PADDLE_W / 2, PADDLE_Y, PADDLE_W, PADDLE_H);

    // Ball.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, BALL_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Bricks ${bricksLeft}  ·  Best ${highScore}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "lost" || phase === "won") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = phase === "won" ? "#52ffae" : "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText(phase === "won" ? "You Win!" : "Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "square";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase === "playing" ? "playing" : "gameover",
      score,
      player: {
        x: paddle.x,
        y: PADDLE_Y + PADDLE_H / 2,
        visible: paddle.x >= 0 && paddle.x <= canvas.width,
      },
      objective: "Bounce the ball with the paddle to break every brick.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Radius Flip
Concept: The dot auto‑advances forward; tapping toggles its size between a tiny point that slips through narrow gaps and a large sphere that smashes any obstacles for a few seconds, after which it returns to normal with a cooldown.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path. If you only described the task list in chat without invoking the tool, the file will not exist on disk and validation will fail. After the file is written, stop. Do not begin implementation.

Examples that have failed in the past: collision check compared a value against itself; \"spike\" collision only clamped position instead of killing the player; obstacles never reached the player.\n107: \n108: ### 2. The player can score\n109: \n110: The score must increase on a **discrete event** (a survived obstacle, a collected pickup, an enemy destroyed, a beat hit). \"Score = elapsed seconds\" is allowed but weak. The first 3 seconds are survivable with no input\n113: \n114: Define a `SAFE_RADIUS` (e.g. 200px) around the player's starting position. When spawning hazards, **reject any spawn that is within `SAFE_RADIUS` of the player AND would reach them within 3 seconds at current hazard speed**. Pre-spawning hazards on top of the player at t=0 has caused frame-1 deaths in past games. Don't do it.\n115: \n116: ### 4. The control actually changes the outcome\n117: \n118: Whatever the player presses must, in the next frame, change a value that affects collision or score. If a control is purely cosmetic (e.g. \"stretch horizontally\" but the gaps are vertical), the game is fundamentally broken even if it runs. That function MUST `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` the previous loop before scheduling a new one — otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game slows to a crawl over time.\n142: \n143: ```js\n144: let loopId = null;\n145: function startLoop() {\n146:   if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);\n147:   function tick(now) { /* ... */ loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); }\n148:   loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n149: }\n150: ```\n151: \n152: ### 7.
